There are 5 steps to deploying a new native spot asset. Note: Each step is permanent. You cannot change any inputs after proceeding to the next step.
The gas paid for deploying a spot token is determined by a Dutch auction once every 31 hours.
At any point, a deployer may pay the current gas price to deploy a token, after which there can be no more deploys for the remainder of the auction period. Gas is deducted from the deployer's spot HYPE balance.
Lowest price, order size, number of orders and number of seeded levels must be specified.
Hyperliquidity commits permanent liquidity to the order book, which will run an onchain strategy in perpetuity. It is similar to burning the LP tokens of initial Uniswap pool liquidity.
Total supply would be too large even at smallest possible Hyperliquidity initial price

Distribute the amount specified below proportionally to holder of an existing token. The token is specified as its integer ID. For example, PURR is the integer "1"
Once genesis is triggered below, Hyperliquidity will activate and users will be able to trade immediately on the spot book.
